<h3 class="_contentTitle_1w20y_202 _title_1rvst_87 _size:headline-4_s02wv_248 _markdown_s02wv_403">Chrome <b>Auto Browse</b></h3>
<div class="_contentBody_1w20y_166 _body_1rvst_92 _size:body_s02wv_297">

<p>Available to Google AI Pro and Ultra users in the US.</p>

<section id="project-genie" class="_section_13e8s_84" aria-label="Create and explore infinitely diverse worlds">
<div class="_spacer_xtoak_74 _size:R14_xtoak_393" role="separator" aria-hidden="true"></div>
<div id class="_headline_1bg32_75 _theme:dark_1bg32_219 _title:text-wrap-balance_1bg32_213 _title:gradient-ai_1bg32_148 _container_1jejk_75" style="--title-max-width:980px;--body-max-width:800px;">
<div class="_content_1bg32_87">
<div class="_eyebrow_1bg32_104 _eyebrowTag_xpp81_75 _theme:dark_xpp81_90 _variant:filled_xpp81_94 _size:eyebrow_s02wv_290">New</div>
<h2 class="_title_1bg32_107 _size:headline-1_s02wv_164 _markdown_s02wv_403">Create and explore <b>infinitely diverse worlds</b></h2>
<div class="_body_1bg32_116 _size:body_s02wv_297">
<p><a href="https://deepmind.google/blog/genie-3-a-new-frontier-for-world-models/">Project Genie</a> is an early research prototype of a world model that lets you create interactive environments, build characters, and explore landscapes that generate themselves in real-time.</p>

<div class="_content_1bg32_87">
<h2 class="_title_1bg32_107 _size:headline-2_s02wv_192 _markdown_s02wv_403">Frequently asked questions</h2>

<div class="_spacer_xtoak_74 _size:R10_xtoak_309" role="separator" aria-hidden="true"></div><g1-faq id="faq" class="_theme:dark_wx4f2_224 _blue-links_wx4f2_240" data-event-params="{&quot;pagePosition&quot;:&quot;body&quot;,&quot;moduleHeadline&quot;:&quot;Frequently asked questions&quot;}">

<mws-accordion-item id="faq-faq-0" class="_faq_wx4f2_79 _accordionItem_n1ciw_79" data-event-label="What is included in Google AI plans?"><button id="faq-faq-0-trigger" class="_accordionItemTrigger_n1ciw_82" aria-controls="faq-faq-0-content" data-slot="accordion-item-trigger"><span>What is included in Google AI plans?</span>
<div class="_expandIcon_wx4f2_94">
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
</div>
</button>
<div id="faq-faq-0-content" class="_accordionItemContentWrap_n1ciw_119" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-faq-0-trigger">
<div class="_accordionItemContent_n1ciw_119" data-slot="accordion-item-content">
<div class="_faqAnswerBlocks_wx4f2_163">
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="RichTextBlock">
<div class="_richTextBlock_1e9tk_75">
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297">
<p>There are three Google AI plans that users can subscribe to: Google AI Plus, Google AI Pro, and Google AI Ultra.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="RichTextBlock">
<div class="_richTextBlock_1e9tk_75">
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297">
<p>Google AI Plus gives you more access to Google AI, including:</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="ListBlock">
<div class="_listBlock_1dpft_75">
<ul>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Gemini app with more access to 3.1 Pro and limited access to Veo 3.1 Fast<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a></p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Flow with more access to Veo 3.1<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a></p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>NotebookLM with more access</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Google Search:<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 5" href="#footnote:search"><sup>5</sup></a> Get more access to the Gemini 3 Pro model to unlock interactive simulations and tools to answer your most complex questions, and more in AI Mode</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Gemini in Gmail and more</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>200 GB of storage for Google Photos, Drive, and Gmail</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Other <a href="https://one.google.com/about/plans?g1_landing_page=0">member benefits</a></p>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="RichTextBlock">
<div class="_richTextBlock_1e9tk_75">
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297">
<p>Google AI Pro gives you everything in Plus, with higher access to Google AI, and:</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="ListBlock">
<div class="_listBlock_1dpft_75">
<ul>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Gemini app with higher limits to 3.1 Pro and access to Veo 3.1 Fast<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a></p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Flow with higher limits to Veo 3.1<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a></p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>NotebookLM with higher access</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Google Search:<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 5" href="#footnote:search"><sup>5</sup></a> Get access to the Gemini 3 Pro model to unlock interactive simulations and tools to answer your most complex questions, plus Deep Search, agentic capabilities, and more</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Jules:<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 6" href="#footnote:jules"><sup>6</sup></a> Higher task and concurrency limits, and access to latest models</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Gemini Code Assist and Gemini CLI: Higher daily request limits in Gemini CLI and Gemini Code Assist IDE extensions</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Google Antigravity: Higher rate limits to agent model in Google Antigravity, our agentic development platform</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>More Remix generations and Veo 3<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a> photo-to-video generations in Google Photos (US only)</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Gemini in Gmail, Docs, Vids, and more</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Google Home Premium Standard: 30 days of event history and Gemini features. Included in all markets where Google Home Premium is available. <a href="https://support.google.com/store/answer/2462844">Learn more</a></p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>2 TB of storage for Google Photos, Drive, and Gmail</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Other premium <a href="https://one.google.com/about/plans?g1_landing_page=0">member benefits</a></p>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="RichTextBlock">
<div class="_richTextBlock_1e9tk_75">
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297">
<p>Google AI Ultra gives you the highest level of access to everything in Google AI Pro, as well as:</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="ListBlock">
<div class="_listBlock_1dpft_75">
<ul>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Gemini app with highest limits to 3.1 Pro and Veo 3.1<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a>, plus access to Deep Think and Gemini Agent (US only, English only)</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Flow with highest limits to Veo 3.1<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a></p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Project Mariner<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 7" href="#footnote:mariner"><sup>7</sup></a> (US only)</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Project Genie<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 2" href="#footnote:genie"><sup>2</sup></a> (US only)</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>YouTube Premium<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 8" href="#footnote:ytp"><sup>8</sup></a> individual plan</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Google Search:<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 5" href="#footnote:search"><sup>5</sup></a> Highest access to Gemini 3 Pro model to unlock interactive simulations and tools to answers your most complex questions, plus Deep Search, agentic capabilities and more</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Jules:<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 6" href="#footnote:jules"><sup>6</sup></a> Highest task and concurrency limits, and access to latest models</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Gemini Code Assist and Gemini CLI: Highest daily request limits in Gemini CLI and Gemini Code Assist IDE extensions</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Google Antigravity: Highest rate limits to agent model in Google Antigravity, our agentic development platform</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Highest Remix generations and Veo 3<a class="_footnoteButton_18w2q_75" aria-label="Show footnote 4" href="#footnote:videogen"><sup>4</sup></a> photo-to-video generations in Google Photos (US only)</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Google Home Premium Advanced: 24/7 video history, event descriptions, and more. Included in all markets where Google Home Premium is available. <a href="https://support.google.com/store/answer/2462844">Learn more</a></p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>30 TB of storage for Google Photos, Drive, and Gmail</p>
</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class="_listItemBody_1dpft_79 _size:body-alt_s02wv_311 _weight:medium_s02wv_388">
<p>Other premium <a href="https://one.google.com/about/plans?g1_landing_page=0">member benefits</a></p>
</div>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297" data-type="RichTextBlock">
<div class="_richTextBlock_1e9tk_75">
<div class="_size:body_s02wv_297">
<p>Google AI Ultra is available in more than 140 countries — see <a href="https://support.google.com/gemini/answer/16275805#ultra_countries">full list of countries</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>

<mws-accordion-item id="faq-faq-1" class="_faq_wx4f2_79 _accordionItem_n1ciw_79" data-event-label="Can I share the AI benefits included in Google AI plans?"><button id="faq-faq-1-trigger" class="_accordionItemTrigger_n1ciw_82" aria-controls="faq-faq-1-content" data-slot="accordion-item-trigger"><span>Can I share the AI benefits included in Google AI plans?</span>
<div class="_expandIcon_wx4f2_94">
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
</div>
</button>
<div id="faq-faq-1-content" class="_accordionItemContentWrap_n1ciw_119" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-faq-1-trigger">
<div class="_accordionItemContent_n1ciw_119" data-slot="accordion-item-content">
<div class="_faqAnswer_wx4f2_150">
<p>Family plan members on a Google AI Pro plan can enjoy AI benefits and features at no extra cost. <a href="https://support.google.com/googleone/answer/9004015">Learn more</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</mws-accordion-item>
</div>
<div class="_faqItemWrap_wx4f2_91">

<mws-accordion-item id="faq-faq-2" class="_faq_wx4f2_79 _accordionItem_n1ciw_79" data-event-label="I’m a Google Workspace customer, can I sign up for Google AI plans?"><button id="faq-faq-2-trigger" class="_accordionItemTrigger_n1ciw_82" aria-controls="faq-faq-2-content" data-slot="accordion-item-trigger"><span>I’m a Google Workspace customer, can I sign up for Google AI plans?</span>
<div class="_expandIcon_wx4f2_94">
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
</div>
</button>
<div id="faq-faq-2-content" class="_accordionItemContentWrap_n1ciw_119" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-faq-2-trigger">
<div class="_accordionItemContent_n1ciw_119" data-slot="accordion-item-content">
<div class="_faqAnswer_wx4f2_150">
<p>Only personal Google Accounts can sign up for Google AI plans. Workspace customers can sign up for a Gemini add-on to their existing subscription. <a href="https://workspace.google.com/solutions/ai/">Learn more</a> about which offering works best for you and your business.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</mws-accordion-item>
</div>
<div class="_faqItemWrap_wx4f2_91">

<mws-accordion-item id="faq-faq-3" class="_faq_wx4f2_79 _accordionItem_n1ciw_79" data-event-label="What countries are Google AI plans available in?"><button id="faq-faq-3-trigger" class="_accordionItemTrigger_n1ciw_82" aria-controls="faq-faq-3-content" data-slot="accordion-item-trigger"><span>What countries are Google AI plans available in?</span>
<div class="_expandIcon_wx4f2_94">
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
</div>
</button>
<div id="faq-faq-3-content" class="_accordionItemContentWrap_n1ciw_119" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-faq-3-trigger">
<div class="_accordionItemContent_n1ciw_119" data-slot="accordion-item-content">
<div class="_faqAnswer_wx4f2_150">
<p>Google AI Pro is available in over 150 countries. Google AI Ultra is available in more than 140 countries — see <a href="https://support.google.com/gemini/answer/16275805#ultra_countries">full list of countries</a>.</p>
<p>Gemini app and Gemini in Gmail, Docs, and more in Google AI plans are only available for ages 18+. Gemini in Gmail, Docs, and more is available in select languages — see <a href="https://support.google.com/docs/answer/14925782">full list of languages</a>.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</mws-accordion-item>
</div>
<div class="_faqItemWrap_wx4f2_91">

<mws-accordion-item id="faq-faq-4" class="_faq_wx4f2_79 _accordionItem_n1ciw_79" data-event-label="How do I use the AI benefits included with Google AI plans?"><button id="faq-faq-4-trigger" class="_accordionItemTrigger_n1ciw_82" aria-controls="faq-faq-4-content" data-slot="accordion-item-trigger"><span>How do I use the AI benefits included with Google AI plans?</span>
<div class="_expandIcon_wx4f2_94">
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
</div>
</button>
<div id="faq-faq-4-content" class="_accordionItemContentWrap_n1ciw_119" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-faq-4-trigger">
<div class="_accordionItemContent_n1ciw_119" data-slot="accordion-item-content">
<div class="_faqAnswer_wx4f2_150">
<p>When you start a trial or are subscribed to a Google AI plan, you can use the following AI features:</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p>Gemini app: Access it at&nbsp;<a href="https://gemini.google.com/">gemini.google.com</a> or through the <a href="https://support.google.com/gemini/answer/14579026?hl=en">mobile app</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Generate AI videos in Flow, Gemini, and Whisk.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Gemini in Gmail, Docs, Slides, Sheets, and Meet: Capabilities can be used directly within these Google products.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>NotebookLM: Access it at&nbsp;<a href="https://notebooklm.google/">notebooklm.google</a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</mws-accordion-item>
</div>

<mws-accordion-item id="faq-faq-5" class="_faq_wx4f2_79 _accordionItem_n1ciw_79" data-event-label="Can I create videos with a Google AI plan?"><button id="faq-faq-5-trigger" class="_accordionItemTrigger_n1ciw_82" aria-controls="faq-faq-5-content" data-slot="accordion-item-trigger"><span>Can I create videos with a Google AI plan?</span>
<div class="_expandIcon_wx4f2_94">
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
</div>
</button>
<div id="faq-faq-5-content" class="_accordionItemContentWrap_n1ciw_119" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-faq-5-trigger">
<div class="_accordionItemContent_n1ciw_119" data-slot="accordion-item-content">
<div class="_faqAnswer_wx4f2_150">
<p>Our Google AI plans give you access to video generation in Gemini, Flow, and Whisk to help you bring your ideas in motion.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</mws-accordion-item>
</div>
<div class="_faqItemWrap_wx4f2_91">

<mws-accordion-item id="faq-faq-6" class="_faq_wx4f2_79 _accordionItem_n1ciw_79" data-event-label="What is the difference between AI Premium and a Google AI Pro plan?"><button id="faq-faq-6-trigger" class="_accordionItemTrigger_n1ciw_82" aria-controls="faq-faq-6-content" data-slot="accordion-item-trigger"><span>What is the difference between AI Premium and a Google AI Pro plan?</span>
<div class="_expandIcon_wx4f2_94">
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
<div class="_expandIconLine_wx4f2_118"></div>
</div>
</button>
<div id="faq-faq-6-content" class="_accordionItemContentWrap_n1ciw_119" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-faq-6-trigger">
<div class="_accordionItemContent_n1ciw_119" data-slot="accordion-item-content">
<div class="_faqAnswer_wx4f2_150">
<p>The Google AI Premium plan has a new name: Google AI Pro. It includes the same benefits like the Gemini app, NotebookLM, 2 TB of storage, and more.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</mws-accordion-item>
</div>

<footer class="_footer_1q5o3_74 _theme:dark_1q5o3_175 _footer_3xk2q_75 _variant:dark_3xk2q_81">
<div aria-label="Footnotes" class="_footnotes_1q5o3_75 _theme:dark_1q5o3_175 _footnotes_1q5o3_75 _theme:dark_1q5o3_175 _container_1jejk_75">
<ul class="_footnotesList_1q5o3_82 _unstyledList_hscrr_2">
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:ai_plans"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">1. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Google AI Plus, Pro, and Ultra plans are available in more than 140 countries and territories — see <a href="https://support.google.com/gemini/answer/16275805" target="_blank">full list of countries</a>. Certain AI benefits are only available for those aged 18+. <a href="https://support.google.com/googleone/answer/16105039" target="_blank">Learn more</a>.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:genie"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">2. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Project Genie is only available in the US.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:offer"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">3. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Offer can be redeemed once per Google account and may not be combined with other offers.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:videogen"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">4. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Veo 3.1 and Flow are available in more than 140 countries and territories — see full list of countries for <a href="https://support.google.com/gemini/answer/16126339#veo3_countries" target="_blank">Veo 3.1</a> and <a href="https://support.google.com/googleone?p=g1_aip_flow" target="_blank">Flow</a>.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:search"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">5. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Gemini 3 Pro model, Nano Banana Pro, and Deep Search in AI Mode are available in select countries — see <a href="https://support.google.com/websearch/answer/16011537#zippy=%2Csupported-countries-territories">full list of countries with Gemini 3 Pro in AI Mode</a> and <a href="https://support.google.com/websearch/answer/16649374#zippy=%2Csupported-countries-territories">full list of countries with Nano Banana Pro in AI Mode</a></p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:jules"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">6. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Jules is an asynchronous coding agent that is currently in beta. To use Jules, you must be 18 years old or older. Only English is officially supported at this time. Capacity is subject to availability and not guaranteed.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:mariner"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">7. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Project Mariner is only available in the US.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:ytp"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">8. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>YouTube Premium individual plan is included in Google AI Ultra in over 40 countries — see <a href="https://support.google.com/googleone/answer/16286513?ref_topic=12344789&sjid=4377067844429597045-NC#zippy=%2Clist-of-countries-regions-where-the-youtube-premium-benefit-is-available">full list of countries</a>.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:resultsvary"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">9. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Results are for illustrative purposes and may vary. Check responses for accuracy. Availability may vary by country and language.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:homep"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">10. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Requires Google Home app, Wi-Fi, and internet connection. Features subject to change; available in <a href="https://support.google.com/store/answer/2462844">select countries and languages</a>. Gemini features work independently of Gemini apps. Check responses for accuracy; results may vary.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:flow"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">11. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Flow is available in more than 140 countries — see <a href="https://support.google.com/googleone?p=g1_aip_flow">full list of countries</a>.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:ai_credits"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">12. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>AI credits (daily and monthly) are shared across Flow and Whisk.</p>
</span></li>
<li class="_footnote_1q5o3_75" id="footnote:finance"><span class="_footnoteSymbol_1q5o3_103 _size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">13. </span><span class="_size:small-text_s02wv_325 _weight:regular_s02wv_385">
<p>Deep Search in Google Finance is only available in the US.</p>
</span></li>
</ul>
